# EUROPE DEPTH RUN — per-country source ledger

Generated from `data/europe-depth/*-sources.json` by `europe-catalog-docs.ts`
— edit the JSON, not this file. Target: ≥50 genuine business-grade public
sources per country; true counts reported honestly, never padded. Access
methods recorded as probed; blocked routes are documented, not hidden.

This file is the HARVEST PLAN — sources the pipeline ingests. The
competitor / intelligence-provider market map lives separately in
`docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md`; the two are deliberately not mixed.

### AL — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

IMPORTANT METHOD CAVEAT: this session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before the task began, so discovery relied on prior domain knowledge plus ~25 direct WebFetch verifications; a follow-up web-search pass is advisable to catch anything newly launched. Genuinely ABSENT or NOT FOUND for Albania: (1) PE/VC association — none exists; no Invest Europe national association, no Albanian fund-managers association; the GP ecosystem is essentially regional (SEE funds, EBRD/AADF vehicles). (2) Crypto/MiCA/VASP public licensee register — Albania's fintech law 66/2020 created a licensing framework under AMF, but no operational public register of licensed VASPs was located. (3) Carbon registry — Albania is outside the EU ETS; no national carbon registry exists. (4) Valuer directory — no public RICS-style or national online roll of licensed property valuers located (licensing is ministerial, lists not found online). (5) Public lawyer roll — DHKA (bar) site verified but exposes no searchable advocate directory. (6) Stock exchange depth — ALSE is live but lists mostly government securities with extremely few corporate issuers; the older state-backed Tirana Stock Exchange closed (~2017) without ever trading listed equities, so 'issuer list' coverage is honest but near-empty. (7) Insurers association (Shoqata e Siguruesve) — believed to exist but no working URL verified. (8) Startup Albania agency / dedicated startup-media portal — not verified. (9) QKB bulk/open-data or API — none found; only annual/monthly statistics. (10) Machine access blockers: amf.gov.al, bankofalbania.org, ieka.al, ashk.gov.al, qbz.gov.al and most major news sites (panorama, politiko, top-channel, seenews, reporter.al, exit.al, businessmag.al) return 403 to non-browser clients; instat.gov.al and fint.gov.al refused connections; gjykata.gov.al returned 500 — all need browser-grade crawling or re-verification. Total honest count is ~50 entries only if one pads; the genuine catalog above is 49 entries of which ~30 were live-verified this session.

_AT competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (8 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### AT — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but confirmed absent or unusable: (1) a free/open-data bulk dump of the Firmenbuch — does not exist; access is per-query paid via JustizOnline or the 10 authorized Verrechnungsstellen (APIs exist but are paid); (2) Wiener Börse free CSV issuer export — explicitly discontinued by the exchange (HTML lists remain); (3) a public OTS RSS index — /rss/ returns 404; per-channel/keyword RSS appears registration-gated; (4) FMA machine access of any kind — the WAF 403 confirmed on fma.gv.at (company database, MiCAR pages, news alike); ESMA registers + OeNB CSVs + WKO lists are the workarounds; (5) a standalone Austrian carbon-account list — Austrian ETS accounts live inside the EU Union Registry/EUTL, with only guidance at emissionshandelsregister.at; (6) open UBO data — WiEReG public access is restricted post-CJEU (legitimate-interest, fee-based); (7) a dedicated NPL/servicer register — no such AT register exists; the lender universe must be assembled from OeNB lists + FMA licences + Ediktsdatei activity; (8) a separate RICS Austria public directory — Austrian valuers are best covered by the open Gerichtssachverständigenliste; (9) WirtschaftsBlatt (former financial daily) — defunct since 2016; (10) RSS for Die Presse, Kurier, Börse Express, e-fundresearch — all four domains block or refuse our fetcher, so their feeds are recorded as unverified and must be retested from production infrastructure. Web-search budget for this session was exhausted near the end; ÖBAG/aws/OeEB/KSW/RIS rows were verified by direct fetch instead, and items marked 'not fetch-verified' are flagged honestly in their verdicts.

### BA — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

IMPORTANT CAVEAT: the session's WebSearch budget (200/200) was already exhausted before this task started, so discovery ran on direct URL verification (~35 fetches) plus domain knowledge; a follow-up search pass could add cantonal and municipal sources. GENUINELY ABSENT in BA after checking: (1) No national PE/VC association — no BVCA/Invest-Europe member body exists for BiH; the startup/VC layer is only visible via BIT Alliance, regional SEE ecosystem reports and donor programs (EBRD/EIF/World Bank). (2) No carbon registry / EU ETS national accounts — BiH is not in the EU ETS (only Energy Community obligations); no national ETS registry exists. (3) No MiCA register — BiH is not an EU member; the only crypto register is the RS Securities Commission's register of virtual currency service providers (on secrs.gov.ba); FBiH has no equivalent found. (4) No RICS national directory — RICS has no BA-specific public register; valuation professionals appear only as court-appointed experts (sudski vjestaci) on entity justice-ministry lists, for which I could not verify a stable consolidated URL. (5) No unified national UBO register — beneficial-ownership registers exist separately at FIA (FBiH) and APIF (RS) and are authentication-gated, consistent with post-2022 EU case law; no open access. (6) No dedicated national insolvency-register portal — bankruptcy/liquidation notices are published through the entity official gazettes and court announcements on the bizreg portal, not a standalone register. (7) No voluntary-pension-fund register of substance — the LP-side pension layer is essentially PREF RS plus one RS voluntary fund manager; FBiH's voluntary pension market never materialized. (8) No separate fund-administrator licensee list — fund custody/administration licensing sits inside the two securities commissions' custodian-bank registers. ALSO SEARCHED/ATTEMPTED BUT NOT VERIFIABLE THIS SESSION (real institutions, fetch-blocked or unresolved): komvp.gov.ba (403), fba.ba (TLS), nados.ba (timeout), azobih.gov.ba (redirect loop), aod.ba (403), bihkonk.gov.ba (timeout), fipa.gov.ba (redirect loop), RS Bar Association, both notary chambers, Brcko District Finance Directorate standalone site, RS Ministry of Finance press page, and LRC BIS's current domain (lrcbis.com DNS fails).

### BE — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but not available/not verifiable: (1) UBO register — exists at FPS Finance but public access was cut after the Nov 2022 CJEU ruling; only obliged entities/authorities; do not plan on open UBO data (matches project rule). (2) No public register of NPL/credit servicers found yet (Credit Servicers Directive register at NBB expected but not located as a published list). (3) No Belgian litigation-funder register — litigation funding is unregulated in BE; coverage must come from press + KBO. (4) No standalone national insolvency-practitioner roll — curators appear per case in RegSol/Staatsblad, not as an open roster. (5) De Tijd RSS feed URLs — Mediafin help center is closed and tijd.be/lecho.be block fetchers; De Tijd feeds could not be confirmed. (6) De Standaard and Le Soir RSS URLs — sites block automated access, feeds unverified. (7) NBB RSS — nbb.be/en/rss returns 404; no working NBB feed found, and NBB press-page path could not be pinned down (three candidate paths 404). (8) FSMA machine access — every fsma.be fetch was reset (ECONNRESET); all FSMA URLs are search-index-verified only and its registers are largely PDFs, so plan a browser-grade fetcher there. (9) Belgian Leasing Association — sits inside Febelfin, no separate verified member directory. (10) RICS Belgium — no country-level public valuer directory; only the global rics.org find-a-member search. (11) Kanaal Z/Canal Z (business TV), Belga News Agency, and De Rijkste Belgen (wealth/holdings tracker) were identified as candidates but omitted because their URLs/feeds were not verified in this session. Web search budget hit its session cap near the end; all included sources rest on direct fetch verification or search-index corroboration as stated per-source.

### BG — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Constraint disclosed: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task began, so discovery ran on direct URL verification (40+ fetches) plus prior knowledge; every unverified item is flagged in its verdict. Absent or dead despite targeted probing: Association of Collection Agencies in Bulgaria (ACABG) — acabg.bg no longer resolves (DNS dead), so no NPL-servicer directory beyond BNB's Art. 3a financial-institutions register; no standalone Bulgarian leasing or factoring association directory found (both populations are covered via the BNB Art. 3a register and ABB associate members); the pension-companies association (BADDPO) has no confirmed working site — pension-company data flows via FSC instead; FSC's legacy ERiK register endpoints (documented unstable) appear superseded by eis.fsc.bg, which is JS-rendered; no public API for the Commercial Register — bulk only via data.egov.bg (which 403s plain fetchers); no dedicated Bulgarian litigation-funding, royalties or ILS association exists; RICS has no separate Bulgarian directory (global RICS directory plus the CIAB valuer register are the routes); novinite.com and mediapool.bg RSS paths 404'd; investor.bg advertises RSS but the /rss/ path 404s (feed URL must be scraped from the live footer); Capital/Dnevnik feeds exist but are Tollbit-gated for bots.

_CH competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (8 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### CH — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but not found / does not exist for Switzerland: (1) public UBO/beneficial-ownership register — Switzerland has none (the new transparency register under the 2026 federal act will NOT be public); (2) national insolvency-practitioner roll — bankruptcy administration is run by cantonal Konkursämter, no central public register of practitioners; (3) MiCA licensee register — CH is not in the EU; the equivalents are FINMA fintech licences, DLT trading-facility authorisations and SRO membership (all catalogued above); (4) EU ETS national accounts for CH — Switzerland runs its own linked Emissions Trading Registry (catalogued) rather than an EU ETS national account; a downloadable public participant list was not confirmed; (5) open bulk download of the full commercial register — Zefix API is per-query; cantonal ToS prohibit systematic replication for competing registries; (6) working RSS feeds for Handelszeitung, cash.ch, fuw.ch, investrends.ch and immoday.ch — none found (404s/redirect loops); startupticker claims an RSS feed in its about page but no working feed URL could be located; swissinfo's old /eng/rss returns 410; (7) ASIP pension-association public member directory — not published; (8) a national register of pension funds with per-fund investment data — OAK BV publishes only aggregates; per-fund data lives in individual annual reports; (9) a Swiss litigation-funding or royalty-fund register — no such register exists; those entities surface only via Zefix + SECA + press.

### CY — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Constraints first: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so discovery ran on direct URL verification (~40 WebFetch probes, Greek and English paths) over the known Cyprus institutional set; anything listed js-blocked/unknown EXISTS but refused automated fetching. Searched and genuinely absent: (1) No dedicated national PE/VC association — Invest Europe has no Cyprus member association; CIFA (funds) and CYBAN (angels) fill the role. (2) No national development bank or promotional institution — the former Cyprus Development Bank was privatized long ago (cdbbank is commercial); early-stage public capital runs through the EIF-managed Cyprus Equity Fund and RIF grants. (3) No privatization agency — the Privatisation Unit was abolished in 2019; no portal exists. (4) No standalone national or voluntary carbon registry — only the EU Union Registry (Department of Environment as administrator; EUTL is the public data route). (5) No REIT register — Cyprus has no REIT regime. (6) No public UBO register access (post-2022 CJEU restriction, as expected). (7) No RSS anywhere on the official side — CySEC, CSE, CBC, eProcurement and CIFA offer none; on the press side only Cyprus Mail, Politis and Sigmalive have verified working feeds; CBN's feed exists but is empty; Financial Mirror/StockWatch feeds could not be confirmed through their bot walls. (8) No litigation-funding, royalty-fund or ILS-specific Cyprus registers or associations were found — those niches have no dedicated local infrastructure and are covered via CySEC AIF/RAIF registers. (9) Old CKAN API paths on data.gov.cy are dead — the new portal is HTML-search only, but the full company register remains downloadable as monthly CC-BY CSVs. (10) A Cyprus pension-funds association could not be verified this session and is omitted rather than guessed. Count honesty: 62 real sources catalogued; nothing padded.

### CZ — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Web SEARCH budget was exhausted at session start (200/200 used by the parent workflow), so all verification was done by direct URL fetching; local-language search-based discovery of long-tail sources was therefore narrower than intended. Searched/attempted but NOT confirmed: (1) a dedicated ČNB MiCA/CASP licensee page — the guessed URL 404'd; CASPs are licensed by ČNB and should appear in the JERRS lists, but the specific list route needs confirmation; (2) a standalone fund-administrator/depositary licensee list — exists only as roles inside ČNB JERRS, no separate public roll found; (3) RICS Czech Republic member directory — RICS has no public CZ-specific directory URL verified; only the global rics.org 'Find a Surveyor'; the Czech court-expert register (seznat.justice.cz) is the practical valuer roll; (4) ISIR web-service documentation — the WS help link 404'd and the new eISIR (eisir.justice.cz, in testing mode) publishes no API docs yet, so the exact SOAP endpoint must be re-located even though the service is known to exist; (5) Asociace developerů ČR — asociacedeveloperu.cz DNS did not resolve from this environment; (6) iRozhlas economy RSS — guessed feed URL 404'd; (7) Stavební fórum (stavebni-forum.cz) — connection refused; (8) ČAP insurance association — hard WAF block (supin.cz gateway); (9) kurzy.cz RSS index and Hlídač státu swagger — pages exist but yielded no concrete endpoints; (10) CNB ARAD and dataor.justice.cz are JS-shell pages whose bulk/export routes could not be inspected by fetch. NEN (nen.nipez.cz) and apl.cnb.cz JERRS UI refused connections — likely datacenter-IP filtering, so production crawler IPs should retest. Also deliberately absent: open UBO data (esm.justice.cz closed to the public on 2025-12-17 — confirmed on-site), and any Preqin/PitchBook/Dealroom/Crunchbase scraping (prohibited by project law; market-map analysis only).

_DE competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (10 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### DE — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but genuinely not found/confirmed: (1) any official open-data bulk API for the Handelsregister — free web search exists since 2022 but structured XML register data is only available via the paid Registerdatenübermittlung channel; the 2019 OffeneRegister dump is the only free bulk artifact. (2) A working RSS feed for FINANCE Magazin, Börsen-Zeitung, finanz-szene.de (/feed returned 500), private-banking-magazin and dasinvestment (/feed 404) — these must be index-parsed. (3) Direct fetch verification of BaFin's MVP portal (WAF/header error, consistent with known issues), manager-magazin and FAZ RSS (bot-blocked here though feed URLs are documented elsewhere), and finance-magazin.de entirely. (4) A dedicated public register of fund administrators separate from BaFin's InstInfo — none exists; fund-admin licensees only enumerate via BaFin institution-type filters. (5) A German national privatization agency in the CEE sense — closest analogs listed are BImA (federal real estate) and GTAI. (6) Börse Stuttgart/regional exchange issuer lists and INDat (insolvency trade press) were considered but not verified, so they were left out rather than padded in.

_DK competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (10 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### DK — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but not found or not confirmable: (1) Public RSS feeds for any Watch Medier title (FinansWatch/EjendomsWatch/EnergiWatch/KapitalWatch/AdvokatWatch) and for Børsen — none discoverable; these are hard-paywalled and their domains block our fetcher, so headline monitoring needs browser-grade tooling or licensing. (2) A documented public JSON/XHR endpoint behind the Finanstilsynet Virksomhedsregister SPA — the shell verifiably loads data via JS but the endpoint is undocumented; it must be captured live in DevTools (flagged as the single most valuable unlock for the AIFM/ManCo/MiCA layer). (3) Public Statstidende API documentation — the endpoint (api.statstidende.dk) is live but docs are sparse and access is certificate-gated per datavejviser.dk. (4) A statutory public roll of Danish insolvency practitioners — appointments are court-made; Kuratorforeningen membership is the best public proxy. (5) A bulk issuer-list download for Nasdaq Copenhagen on the new nasdaq.com (the old nasdaqomxnordic.com pages now 301 to JS pages; the Nordic membership-list Excel covers members, not issuers). (6) dvca.dk is dead (serves a mismatched webhosting certificate) — the association renamed to Aktive Ejere at aktiveejere.dk. (7) A separate Danish pension regulator — none exists; Finanstilsynet supervises pensions. (8) A Danish national voluntary carbon registry beyond the EU ETS Union Registry (Erhvervsstyrelsen-administered). (9) Erhvervsstyrelsen's kvoteregister page and ens.dk EU ETS pages returned 403/404 to our fetcher, so the exact Danish ETS-administration URL is unconfirmed. (10) Session constraint: the web-search budget was exhausted mid-run and several major dailies (Berlingske, Politiken, Finans.dk, Børsen) plus TechSavvy block automated fetching, so their RSS status is honestly marked unknown/js-blocked rather than assumed.

### EE — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but genuinely absent or consolidated elsewhere: (1) an active privatization/state-asset sales agency — none exists; large-scale privatization ended in the 1990s (Erastamisagentuur dissolved), state holdings are governed via the Ministry of Finance/Ministry of Economic Affairs ownership reports on fin.ee; (2) a standalone leasing/factoring association — Estonian leasing companies are represented within Pangaliit (Banking Association), which carries leasing statistics; (3) an RICS Estonia national directory — RICS has no Estonian chapter directory, only the global find-a-member; the national valuer route is the Kutseregister qualifications register + EKHÜ; (4) a dedicated fund-administrator or depositary licensee list separate from Finantsinspektsioon — fund admins/depositaries appear only inside FI's supervised-entities register; (5) litigation-funding, royalty, or ILS registers — none exist in Estonia; (6) a standalone NPL/servicer register — debt collectors and creditors fall under FI's creditor/credit-intermediary register and (historically) MTR; (7) a separate insolvency court register — insolvency publication is consolidated into Ametlikud Teadaanded, which is why it is the load-bearing tier3 source. Process caveats stated honestly: the session's WebSearch budget was exhausted after two queries, so RSS discovery for Äripäev, Postimees, Delfi and Geenius relied on direct feed-URL fetches, several of which returned 403 bot-blocks — those feeds are marked js-blocked with candidate rssUrls rather than confirmed; rahandusministeerium.ee is dead (moved to fin.ee) and maaamet.ee now redirects to maaruum.ee (agency renamed 2025); MTR is mid-migration to the new Tarvik system (since 25.06.2025) and crypto supervision moved from FIU to Finantsinspektsioon under MiCA (transition ended 01.07.2026) — both successor routes should be tracked at ingest time.

### FI — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Session web-search budget was exhausted at task start (200/200 used), so discovery ran on domain knowledge + direct URL verification (30+ fetches); items below are stated with that caveat. (1) No standalone Finnish insolvency-practitioner roll: bankruptcy administrators are court-appointed attorneys supervised by Konkurssiasiamies — the Bar Association directory + Konkurssiasiamies are the substitutes. (2) No separate fund-administrator license category: administration falls under FIN-FSA investment-services/AIFM authorizations. (3) No standalone national MiCA register page verified: MiCA licensees surface inside FIN-FSA's supervised-entities/notification lists. (4) RICS has minimal Finland presence: the national valuer credential is Keskuskauppakamari's AKA/KHK authorization, not RICS. (5) Good News from Finland is discontinued as a standalone outlet (301s into businessfinland.com marketing) — dropped rather than padded. (6) Alma Media (Kauppalehti/Talouselämä/Arvopaperi) has withdrawn public RSS and hard-blocks cloud fetchers; legacy feed URLs recorded as unverified. (7) Nasdaq's legacy nordic listed-companies page (nasdaqomxnordic.com) is retired — issuer lists now only via JS-heavy nasdaq.com or disclosure wires. (8) No Finnish privatization agency exists (no active privatization program); state-asset routes are Solidium + PMO ownership steering. (9) No dedicated Finnish litigation-funding or royalties registry found — category 5 sourcing must come via press wires and PRH classification. (10) avoindata.fi and valtioneuvosto.fi/energiavirasto.fi 403-block cloud user agents — real sources, need production-crawler UA, flagged honestly in verdicts.

### FR — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched but not found or excluded to keep the count honest: (1) direct AGEFI RSS feed URLs — the site blocks bot fetches and only third-party feed directories attest feeds exist, so no rssUrl recorded; (2) any ORIAS bulk/open-data export — only the per-record search UI; (3) a confirmed full bulk dump of the merged REGAFI register — daily search UI plus ACPR downloadable lists only; (4) a separate French fund-administrator/depositary licensee register — that population lives inside REGAFI (credit institutions/depositaries) and AMF lists, no standalone roll; (5) Private Equity Magazine and L'Informé — both real publications but I could not confirm their exact domains this session, so they were excluded rather than guessed; (6) a downloadable RICS France valuer roster — only the JS-heavy global Find-a-Member UI; (7) verified RSS for La Tribune, Le Monde Économie, Business Immo, BFM Business, Usine Nouvelle — sites bot-blocked or feeds unconfirmed, marked accordingly rather than invented; (8) a public UBO (bénéficiaires effectifs) feed — INPI RBE access is restricted post-2022 CJEU ruling, consistent with the project's no-open-UBO assumption.

### GB — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

AltFi is dead — fetch of altfi.com/rss returned a closure announcement ('we are closing the AltFi business'), so the hinted source is removed rather than padded. EG/Estates Gazette (LexisNexis) ceased publication, so it is excluded despite being the historic RE trade paper. No working RSS found for IPE (/feed 404s) or for The Pensions Regulator/BoE (BoE 403s bots). No free machine-readable LSE issuer list — the reports page is a JS app returning only a title; Investegate + FCA NSM are the workaround. The Investment Association's members page has moved (old path 404s) and was not relocated this run. Peel Hunt research (hinted) is not public — broker research sits behind MiFID walls (Research Tree is paid), so no entry was made. BVCA's own domain now 301s to ukprivatecapital.co.uk and the full member directory is paid; only the public quick-search tier is catalogued. UK note contra the usual EU rule: PSC (UBO) data IS genuinely public here via Companies House bulk — an exception worth exploiting, not assuming elsewhere.

### GR — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Session constraint disclosed: the WebSearch budget was exhausted before this task began, so discovery was WebFetch-verification of known/hinted URLs rather than fresh search fan-out. Absent or unreachable despite attempts: (1) no public Greek UBO register route — Central UBO Registry access is restricted post-CJEU; (2) no standalone fund-administrator licensee list — administration falls under HCMC/BoG perimeters, and HCMC is geo-blocked (connection refused from non-GR IPs); (3) no Greek litigation-funding register or association found; (4) solvency.gov.gr (insolvency register) did not resolve via DNS from this environment and keyd.gov.gr (insolvency-administrator roll) refused connections — both need a Greek IP; (5) no working RSS could be confirmed for Kathimerini, eKathimerini, Capital.gr, Euro2day, Naftemporiki, AMNA, or Athens Exchange — all bot-blocked (403) or 404 on standard feed paths, though the outlets themselves are real; (6) no public register of occupational pension funds (TEA) holdings — only the National Actuarial Authority (403 to bots) and Ministry of Labour PDFs; (7) no dedicated Greek leasing-association web directory verified (association exists but no reliable URL confirmed, so it was excluded rather than padded); (8) Bank of Greece domain (all registers + press) returns 403 to automated fetchers — content exists but needs browser-grade crawling; (9) GEMI exposes no advertised public bulk API on the publicity portal — data.gov.gr API is the closest open bulk route.

### HR — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Important honesty notes: (1) The session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so discovery was done entirely by direct WebFetch verification of the known source landscape — a search-driven sweep for long-tail sources (municipal portals, niche sector media) was not possible. (2) sudreg-podaci.gov.hr from the hint does NOT resolve; the live open-data host is sudreg-data.gov.hr (fetch hit an auth-portal redirect loop; API requires OAuth2 credentials). (3) Privredni vjesnik: privredni.hr turned out to be an unrelated lifestyle site — the historic business weekly appears to have no verifiable current home; excluded rather than padded. (4) No public UBO register access assumed (Registar stvarnih vlasnika is restricted post-CJEU) — excluded per project rules. (5) No dedicated public register of NPL servicers/credit-purchasers was verified (the NPL secondary-market directive register should sit with HNB — needs a follow-up check); no litigation-funding registry exists in Croatia. (6) Lider has no discoverable RSS (feed 404s, no rel=alternate); Index.hr's business-only feed exists but returned an empty channel. (7) Not fetched this session (flagged unverified in-line): e-Predmet, land-registry OSS, HJK notaries, RICS firms directory, EUTL carbon accounts, CRANE, Večernji biznis, HINA, and the Ministry of Justice register of court-appointed appraisers (sudski vještaci).

### HU — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Process note: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task began, so discovery used the hint list + domain knowledge, verified by ~30 direct URL fetches; verdicts state per-source verification status. Searched-for but absent or unconfirmable: (1) a standalone open Hungarian insolvency register portal — insolvency/liquidation publication flows through the Cégközlöny (which 403-blocks non-browser clients) and the EU e-Justice interconnection; (2) the liquidator roll (felszámolói névjegyzék) direct URL — fni.im.gov.hu does not resolve; current home under IM/OCCSZ needs browser discovery; (3) any public bulk API for the company register — bulk data is sold under contract by the Céginformációs Szolgálat, not open; (4) open UBO data — none, consistent with EU-wide restriction; (5) RSS feeds for MNB, BÉT, MFB, ÁKK, Property Forum — none found, HTML polling required; (6) a standalone Hungarian national carbon-registry portal — the public route is the EU Transaction Log; the national ETS administrator sits under the energy ministry without an obvious open register; (7) a dedicated Hungarian NPL-servicer association — NPL/collections entities appear only via MNB licensing categories (követeléskezelő) in the Intézménykereső; (8) MNV and Hiventures and the ministry Céginformációs page all failed TLS verification from this client — real sites, retry with a browser-grade crawler; (9) Napi.hu and G7.hu no longer exist as separate outlets (301 redirects to Economx and Telex respectively).

### IE — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but absent or unusable: (1) Fora.ie — defunct since 2020, folded into TheJournal.ie (its verified feed is the successor). (2) Any bulk/open download of Central Bank of Ireland registers — the Downloads page exists but robots.txt (fetched and verified) disallows ALL bots incl. ClaudeBot on search, data AND download pages; per hard rule this is documented-only, with ESMA/EBA registers as the machine route. (3) A corporate insolvency-practitioner licensing register — Ireland does not license liquidators/receivers; only ISI's personal-insolvency practitioner (PIP) register exists. (4) A litigation-funding register — third-party funding remains largely prohibited (limited 2023 arbitration carve-out), so no licensee register exists. (5) Public RBO beneficial-ownership access — restricted to legitimate-interest applicants post-CJEU. (6) Working RSS for Irish Times, Business Post, Irish Independent and Irish Examiner — all tested endpoints 403'd or 404'd; legacy feed URLs circulating on aggregators could not be verified. (7) gov.ie RSS — none exists; press indexes are HTML-only. (8) A standalone Irish MiCA register page usable by bots — CBI's version sits on the robots-blocked registers site; ESMA's weekly CSV interim register is the substitute. (9) An open occupational-pension-scheme register from the Pensions Authority — only the PRSA providers PDF and aggregate statistics are published.

_IS competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (6 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### IS — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

METHOD CAVEAT: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so discovery ran entirely on direct URL verification (WebFetch) of known institutions plus the sedlabanki.is sitemap; a broader keyword sweep in Icelandic could not be run. Findings below are what was searched-for/checked and NOT found or not confirmable. (1) No separate private-equity association exists — Framvís (VC/angels) is the only fund-investor association; no BVCA-style PE body found. (2) No standalone insolvency-practitioner roll: trustees (skiptastjórar) are court-appointed lawyers; the only public routes are the Lögmannafélag directory and Lögbirtingablað notices. (3) No dedicated insolvency register: bankruptcy publicity flows through Lögbirtingablað and district courts, not a searchable national insolvency database. (4) No open-data bulk/API route for fyrirtækjaskrá: opingogn.is now redirects to a Digital Iceland page stating the open-data framework is 'being modernized / new arrangement in development' — no dataset catalog or company-register bulk download currently public. (5) FME's standalone site is gone (merged into the Central Bank 2020); all sedlabanki.is register pages exist (sitemap-confirmed) but render empty to non-JS fetchers — headless crawling required. (6) Nasdaq Iceland static issuer lists are gone: nasdaqomxnordic.com redirects to JS-rendered nasdaq.com pages; no parseable public issuer list found. (7) Kjarninn and Fréttablaðið are defunct (Kjarninn+Stundin merged into Heimildin 2023; Fréttablaðið closed 2023) — hint corrected. (8) Nýsköpunarsjóður atvinnulífsins (nsa.is) is unreachable (SSL handshake failure); its reported merger into Kría could not be verified this session. (9) No RICS-style valuer directory found; licensed real-estate agents/appraisers are registered with district commissioners (sýslumenn) but no parseable public roll was verified. (10) No standalone crypto/VASP public list confirmed — registration sits under the Central Bank licensing pages (JS-blocked); ESMA's EEA MiCA register is the machine-readable fallback. (11) No sovereign wealth fund / privatization agency: the proposed Þjóðarsjóður was never established; bank sell-downs run via the Ministry of Finance (Bankasýslan was being wound down). (12) National ETS/carbon account registry (Umhverfisstofnun/Climate authority, EU-registry-based) not verified this session — only the private Iceland-based ICR is cataloged. (13) RSS not confirmed for: Viðskiptablaðið (403 bot-block), Iceland Monitor (/feeds/ 404), Stjórnarráðið (402 anomaly), RÚV business-specific sub-feed. (14) HMS (property register) verified to exist but returned HTTP 429 on every attempt — access method unconfirmed. Iceland is a ~380k-person market: the 42 genuine sources above are materially complete for tiers 1–3; padding to 50 would have required inventing or duplicating.

### IT — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but genuinely absent or restricted: (1) a FREE bulk/open-data dump of the full Registro Imprese — does not exist; machine route is the paid InfoCamere Telemaco contract/API, free layer is per-company search only; (2) public UBO register access — the Italian titolare-effettivo register at the Chambers of Commerce is suspended for general public access after the 2022 CJEU ruling and pending Italian litigation (consistent with project rule: never assume open UBO data); (3) a separate fund-administrator licensee list — Italy has no standalone fund-admin license; depositaries and ManCos sit inside the Banca d'Italia albi; (4) a national machine-readable bar roll — lawyer albi are held per local Ordine; CNF-level bulk download not found; (5) official public RSS for Milano Finanza, CONSOB, We Wealth and Focus Risparmio — hunted /rss, /feed variants, none confirmed working; (6) a national carbon account-holder bulk list from ISPRA — account-level EU ETS data is published at EU level (EUTL), not by the Italian administrator; (7) PVP insolvency-auction open-data/API for the general public — interoperability API exists but only for accredited advertising sites. Nothing in the catalog below is padded: every URL was either fetched directly or returned by search as the canonical page; unverified ones are flagged in their verdicts.

### LI — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Liechtenstein is a micro-market (~40k people); 32 genuine sources were cataloged and the 50 target is NOT reachable without padding. Searched for and confirmed ABSENT or dead: (1) Central bank — none exists; LI is in a currency union with Switzerland (CHF, SNB); monetary/banking statistics come via FMA and Amt für Statistik. (2) Domestic stock exchange — none; the two listed LI issuers (LLB, VP Bank) trade on SIX Swiss Exchange (cataloged). (3) National PE/VC association — none; GPs organize via LAFV, VuVL or the Swiss SECA. (4) Development bank, privatization agency, sovereign wealth fund — none exist. (5) Standalone investment-promotion agency with company data — none; the function sits with LIHK/Liechtenstein Marketing/Amt für Volkswirtschaft. (6) Public UBO access — the beneficial-owner directory (Verzeichnis wirtschaftlich berechtigter Personen) is access-restricted, and per Aug-2026 press it just suffered an unauthorized-access incident; do not assume access. (7) Insolvency-practitioner roll — none; Masseverwalter are appointed case-by-case by the Landgericht; track via eAmtsblatt edicts. (8) RICS/valuer directory for LI — none found (Swiss RICS bodies cover the region). (9) Notaries — profession does not exist in LI. (10) Handelsregister open data/bulk/API — none exists; oera.li (legacy register domain) is unreachable (ECONNRESET twice); workarounds are eAmtsblatt publications + GLEIF LEI API. (11) Liechtensteiner Volksblatt — CEASED publication (2023); volksblatt.li now serves an expired TLS certificate. (12) Radio Liechtenstein — shut down after the 2024 funding referendum; site is a 'Sendeschluss' archive. (13) Crypto Country Association (CCA) — cca.li no longer resolves in DNS; apparently defunct. (14) National carbon-registry portal — none; LI participates in the EU ETS via the EEA Union Registry (cataloged). (15) RSS feeds — hunted on vaterland.li, wirtschaftregional.li, 1fl.li, fma-li.li, bankenverband.li: none found (all 404 on standard feed paths or newsletter-only); the ONLY confirmed working news RSS is lie-zeit.li/feed/ (validated), plus the eAmtsblatt's in-app category subscription feeds. (16) statistikportal.li and llv.li administration pages return HTTP 403 to automated clients; thk.li likewise 403; liechtenstein-finance.li serves a mismatched TLS cert (use finance.li). NOTE: this session's WebSearch budget was exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so discovery relied on direct URL verification via fetch (24 URLs checked) plus the task hints; a follow-up pass with live web search could confirm the two cross-border media feeds and probe FMA/LAFV endpoint details, but is unlikely to surface additional domestic institutions — the ones above are the market.

### LT — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Caveat: this session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so discovery ran on domain knowledge plus direct URL verification via fetch; 54 real sources catalogued, ~28 fetch-verified. Many Lithuanian state domains (e-tar.lt, avnt.lt, teismai.lt, all *.lrv.lt, data.gov.lt, osp.stat.gov.lt, atvira.sodra.lt, invega.lt, ilte.lt, turtas.lt) returned 403/refused to the US-based fetcher — they are real and known-good but need harvesting from an EU IP; marked honestly per row. Searched-for but absent or subsumed: (1) Valstybės žinios — ceased 2014, fully replaced by TAR (e-tar.lt); (2) no separate fund-administrator licensee list — fund administration falls inside the Bank of Lithuania FMP directory categories; (3) no standalone MiCA/crypto register — CASP authorisations sit inside the same LB FMP directory (the pre-MiCA 'crypto operator' JAR-notification list was retired); (4) no Lithuanian NPL-servicer or debt-purchaser association exists (servicers appear via LB consumer-credit/credit-servicer lists); (5) no litigation-funding association or local funder of note; (6) no local RICS chapter directory — only the global ricsfirms.com search plus AVNT's national valuers roll; (7) an independent Lithuanian leasing association could not be confirmed as currently operating (leasing statistics are published by LBA and Bank of Lithuania); (8) RSS endpoints for vz.lt, delfi.lt, lrt.lt and madeinvilnius.lt could not be confirmed because those domains block automated fetchers — only 15min.lt and baltictimes.com feeds were positively verified working.

### LU — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but not found/confirmed: (1) working RSS feeds for Paperjam and Delano — /rss and /feed both 404; Maison Moderne appears to have moved distribution to newsletters; (2) a public ABBL member-directory URL — site live but /en/members/ 404; (3) ALFI membership directory — alfi.lu refused connections from this environment, unverified; (4) a single full-register RCS bulk dump — data.public.lu hosts RCS datasets and LBR has an open-data API, but the exact bulk dataset pages are JS-rendered and were not individually confirmed; (5) open UBO data — RBE access is restricted post-CJEU (expected, per project rule); (6) a confirmed current LuxSE RSS feed URL (one was announced historically); (7) Luxembourg Times and RTL feeds — both hosts blocked plain fetch; (8) any LU-specific litigation-funding, royalty, or private-carbon registry — none exists; carbon route is the EU Union Registry/EUTL with Administration de l'environnement as national administrator; (9) CSSF /en/document-type/list/ and /en/supervised-entities/ guessed URLs — 404; correct routes are the Publication & Data library and the edesk Search Entities SPA.

### LV — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Method note: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so discovery ran on domain knowledge + direct URL verification via fetch; every listed source was fetched, and fetch failures are marked honestly in verdicts. Searched/checked but absent or unconfirmed: (1) LVCA member roster page — association verified live but the members nav link mispoints (Wix); roster needs a crawl retry. (2) manapensija.lv statistics — the /en/statistics page is 404; 2nd-pillar plan/manager stats now live in Latvijas Banka's statdb instead. (3) Fintech Latvia Association — fintechlatvia.eu now 301-redirects to bank.lv's fintech-services page; the association appears to have no independent web presence anymore. (4) No Latvia-specific RICS directory — RICS firms are only searchable via global ricsfirms.com (not verified); the national valuer body (LĪVA, Latvian Association of Property Appraisers) could not be URL-verified this session. (5) No dedicated fund-administrator licensee list — fund administration falls under Latvijas Banka's ManCo/AIFM segments. (6) No separate NPL-servicer register — debt-recovery firms are licensed by PTAC, whose register page could not be located by fetch (agency verified). (7) LVĢMC's EU ETS national-administrator role could not be confirmed on its JS-only site; EUTL at EU level is the fallback for LV ETS accounts. (8) Insolvency-administrator roll at MKD — guessed URLs 404; administrators are surfaced via the Insolvency Register data instead. (9) Bot-blocked (403/JS) but real: advokatura.lv, lursoft.lv, lsm.lv RSS, arcticstartup.com feed, labsoflatvia.com, maksatnespeja.ur.gov.lv, delfi.lv (whole domain), forbesbaltics.com (DNS timeout). (10) No public UBO bulk data — EU-wide public access restriction (expected; project rule already assumes this). No padding was used: 52 catalogued sources are all real and individually checked.

_ME competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (8 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### ME — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Montenegro is a ~620k-population market; 41 genuine sources were found and honesty overrides the 50 target. Note: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted when this task started, so discovery relied on direct URL verification (WebFetch) of known institutions plus the task hints; every 'verified' claim above reflects an actual fetch in this run. Searched for but NOT found / confirmed absent: (1) PE/VC association — no Montenegrin private equity or venture capital association exists (no national member of Invest Europe); the ecosystem proxy is the Innovation Fund + ICT Cortex. (2) CRPS bulk/open-data/API route — data.gov.me carries no business-register dataset; no API documentation found; the register itself (crps.me / pretraga.crps.me) failed TLS handshakes from our tooling (legacy HTTP-only app) so its accessMethod remains unresolved. (3) Dedicated national insolvency portal — none exists; bankruptcy publicity runs through the Commercial Court (inside the JS-rendered sudovi.me portal), the Official Gazette, and CRPS status flags. A standalone roll of licensed insolvency practitioners (stečajni upravnici) is maintained by the Ministry of Justice but no working public URL could be verified this run. (4) Crypto/MiCA/TVTG licensee register — Montenegro is not an EU member; MiCA does not apply, the domestic digital-assets law has not produced an operating public licensee register, and none was found. (5) Carbon registry — no national ETS/carbon-credit registry; Montenegro's emissions scheme runs through the Environment Agency's permitting without a public credits register. (6) Stock-exchange member (broker) list — MNSE's member directory was not separately reachable; broker-dealers are covered via the SCMN investment-firm register instead. (7) Valuer directory — the Institute of Certified Appraisers of Montenegro (IOPCG) domain iopcg.me did not resolve; no working national valuer roll verified (RICS has no ME national association). (8) Privatization agency — the Privatization and Capital Projects Council exists within the Government (gov.me) but has no standalone portal with a machine-accessible asset list. (9) Voluntary pension funds — the third-pillar register sits with the Capital Market Authority; the market has only a handful of entities and no separate regulator. (10) Working RSS could not be confirmed for Vijesti, Bankar.me, Dan, RTCG, MINA, Antena M, or Portal Analitika (403s/bot-blocks/HTML shells) — recorded honestly per source; only CdM, Investitor.me, Balkan Green Energy News, and (dormant) Total Montenegro News feeds were verified working. (11) Fund administrators/depositaries — no local association or public directory; depositary services are provided by CBCG-licensed banks, covered via the CBCG register.

### MK — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Session constraint disclosed honestly: the WebSearch budget was exhausted before this task started (200/200 used), so discovery relied on domain knowledge plus direct URL verification via fetch — every source above marked 'parseable-index', 'rss' or 'js-blocked' was fetch-verified this session; 'unknown' means the site failed verification (DNS/TLS/connection) and needs a re-check. Genuinely absent or not found for North Macedonia: (1) No national PE/VC association exists (no MK member association in Invest Europe; startup ecosystem is organized under Startup Macedonia instead). (2) No AIF/alternative-fund register — SEC publishes only licensed fund-management companies and open-end (UCITS-style) funds; MK has no AIFMD-equivalent public AIF register. (3) No crypto/MiCA/TVTG licensee register — MK is not an EU member, has no crypto licensing regime, and NBRM publishes only warnings, not licenses. (4) No national carbon registry — MK is outside the EU ETS; no domestic voluntary-carbon registry found. (5) Privatization agency — dissolved years ago; no successor register online. (6) No standalone banking association website verified — the banking association operates as an association within the Economic Chamber (mchamber.mk); same for the investment-fund industry grouping. (7) Chamber of certified appraisers/valuers — established by the Law on Appraisal, but no working public directory site could be verified without search; needs a browser session. (8) Register of insolvency trustees (stecajni upravnici) — maintained under the Ministry of Economy/CRM framework, but no standalone public roll was verifiable this session. (9) No REIT regime and no litigation-funding, royalty-fund, or ILS entities found. (10) Verification failures to re-run with a browser: slvesnik.com.mk (TLS misconfiguration), sud.mk and katastar.gov.mk and data.gov.mk (connection refused), fitr.mk (DNS failure — possible domain migration), erc.org.mk / e-nabavki.gov.mk / investnorthmacedonia.gov.mk / seenews.com (HTTP 403 to non-browser fetch). Count honesty: 41 real sources catalogued; padding to 50 was not possible for this market without inventing entries.

### MT — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Constraint disclosed: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task began, so discovery relied on direct URL verification (WebFetch) plus domain knowledge; ~50 URLs were fetch-tested. Genuinely absent or unverifiable after searching/fetching: (1) No Maltese PE/VC association exists (no MVCA-equivalent; Invest Europe has no Malta member association) — the funds-industry association role is filled by FinanceMalta and IFSP; the old Malta Funds Industry Association has no working web presence. (2) Malta Business Angels Network (MBAN) — no live site found; apparently inactive. (3) MARSP (Malta Association of Retirement Scheme Practitioners) — marsp.org.mt timed out (DNS); marsp.org is a Michigan retirees body; treat the Maltese association's web presence as dead pending browser re-check. (4) MIMCOL / privatisation agency — mimcol.com.mt DNS not found; Malta has no active privatization-agency portal; state-holdings news flows through DOI/gov.mt. (5) No separate pension or insurance regulator exists — MFSA is the single regulator (EIOPA register is the machine route); recorded as structure, not absence. (6) No public MBR bulk-download, open-data dump, or developer API found — MBR's API references are government-to-government only. (7) No standalone online insolvency-practitioner roll located — administered by MBR's Insolvency & Receivership Service behind the JS registry. (8) No RICS national association or separate valuer register beyond the Kamra tal-Periti directory. (9) RSS for Times of Malta, MaltaToday, and The Malta Independent could not be verified (domain block / 404s on standard feed paths). (10) No dedicated startup/tech news outlet (Malta startup coverage lives in BusinessNow.mt, Tech.mt news, SiGMA). (11) Malta Stock Exchange member-firm/broker list page could not be pinpointed (guessed paths 404; homepage nav only exposes a generic /links page) — needs browser crawl. (12) No national carbon registry beyond MRA-administered EU Union Registry accounts; MRA's own site fails SSL. Total genuine sources catalogued: 50 — but note several are EU-level mirrors included precisely because the corresponding Maltese portals (MFSA FSR, MBR app) block automation; Malta's true count of distinct national business-grade portals is closer to 40."

### NL — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but could not confirm: (1) a free public BULK download of the AFM registers — only per-register search pages and a daily change-alert email service exist, so AIFM/investment-firm universes must be harvested per register; (2) a free machine-readable Euronext Amsterdam issuer export — the issuer list is a JS app and structured exports sit behind the paid Euronext Data Shop; (3) a public Dutch UBO register — closed to the general public since the Nov 2022 CJEU ruling (access restricted to authorities/obliged entities), consistent with the project's no-open-UBO assumption; (4) working RSS for Consultancy.nl (/rss = 404), IEX.nl (legacy .aspx feed = 404), Pensioen Pro, Energeia, Vastgoedmarkt and PropertyNL — none found; (5) MT/Sprout and Quote feeds — domains bot-blocked our fetcher (403/blocked), feeds unverified; (6) NU.nl RSS unverifiable from this tooling (domain blocked) though the documented feed URL is included; (7) an NVB banking-association public member list page — association confirmed but the list page was not located; (8) a searchable Holland Quaestor member directory — only homepage logos; (9) dnb.nl direct fetches — 403 bot protection across the site, though search results confirm register download files updated each working day; (10) a dedicated Dutch litigation-funder or royalty-fund register — none exists; such entities appear only via KVK/AFM generic registers and press."

### NO — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched but not found or not confirmable: (1) a working public RSS feed for Shifter (both /rss and /rss/ return 404) and for Finansavisen and Estate Nyheter — none located; (2) a separate Norwegian official gazette for company events — Norsk Lysingsblad's company-announcement role was absorbed by Brønnøysund kunngjøringer, which is the actual gazette route; (3) open bulk access to the beneficial-ownership register (register over reelle rettighetshavere) — exists at Brreg but public machine access is still restricted/phased, consistent with the project's no-open-UBO assumption; (4) an open shareholder register — Skatteetaten's aksjonærregister is only released via annual extracts to media/on request, not an open API; (5) machine access to VPS/Euronext Securities holdings — not public. Session constraint: the web-search budget was exhausted near the end, so several association/agency URLs (FinFo, Norsk Eiendom, Tilsynsrådet, AdvokatWatch, NRK feed URL, regjeringen.no feed IDs) are from prior knowledge and marked unverified in their verdicts rather than dropped; the Finanstilsynet crypto/MiCA register was confirmed only as a licence-type filter inside the main FSA registry rather than as a standalone register page.

### PL — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but not found: (1) a Polish public register of MiCA CASP licensees — as of mid-2026 KNF licenses CASPs but publishes no dedicated public CASP register; the ESMA EU-wide register is the authoritative interim source, and the legacy KAS virtual-currency register is being wound down. (2) Working official RSS feeds for the big three dailies — Puls Biznesu, Parkiet, Rzeczpospolita all returned 404 on standard feed paths and legacy feedsportal feeds are dead; their content is paywalled, so headline-level monitoring needs section-page crawling or licensing. (3) A bulk download/open API for KNF registers — e-rup.knf.gov.pl was unreachable (connection refused) and KNF offers only per-register HTML/XLSX pages plus the search UI. (4) A free bulk issuer API from GPW — only HTML lists and PDF/XLS statistics. (5) RSS at cashless.pl, fintek.pl, stockwatch.pl, obligacje.pl standard paths (all 404). (6) A public register of fund administrators as a distinct category — in Poland transfer-agent/fund-admin functions appear only inside KNF's 'entities keeping fund participant registers' pages, not as a standalone licensee list.

### PT — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but absent or unusable: (1) No open/bulk route to the commercial register itself — Portugal has no free bulk company register download or API; only the free per-act publications index (publicacoes.mj.pt) and the paid per-company certidão permanente. (2) RCBE (beneficial-ownership register) exists but public access is restricted post-CJEU — correctly treated as closed per project rules. (3) CMVM legacy machine endpoints (web3.cmvm.pt/sdi and /sdi2004) are dead (404) — the registers now live only behind the JS-blocked main portal; ESMA registers are the working alternate. (4) FNABA (business angels federation) — fnaba.org currently 302-redirects to an unrelated spam domain (wikimoderator.com); no working official site found, so it was excluded. (5) Idealista News RSS (/news/rss) 404s. (6) Público and Expresso feeds could not be confirmed — both domains block automated fetch (403 / connection refused). (7) No dedicated public register of NPL/credit servicers found as a standalone list — credit-servicing licensees sit inside Banco de Portugal's bot-blocked Entidades Autorizadas register. (8) No Portuguese litigation-funding association or dedicated carbon-market association found. (9) APA (EU ETS national administrator) refused connections from our fetcher, and APS (insurers association) loops redirects — both real but unverifiable this run. Note: the session's web-search budget was exhausted at task start, so all verification was done by direct URL fetches; a handful of well-known sources (EBA EUCLID, EIOPA, OROC, DGEG, RICS firms directory, Portugal Fintech) are included with accessMethod marked unknown/api and explicit not-fetched caveats rather than fabricated verdicts.

### RO — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Web-search quota was already exhausted at session start (0 searches available), so discovery relied on domain knowledge verified by direct fetch; several verdicts are marked unverified accordingly. Things looked for and NOT found: (1) any official RSS feed from BNR, ASF, ONRC, or BVB — none advertised; regulator signals require page scraping; (2) an open/free bulk route for ONRC RECOM itself — RECOM stays account-gated/paid, the open slice is the data.gov.ro CSV snapshots; (3) a consolidated Romanian national MiCA/CASP register page at ASF or BNR — not found, ESMA registers are the machine route; (4) a public UBO (beneficial-ownership) access route — consistent with EU-wide restriction, correctly absent; (5) a dedicated NPL-servicer or debt-collection register — these entities live inside the BNR IFN register (recovery/collection IFNs) rather than a standalone roll; (6) working RSS for Wall-Street.ro and Property Forum — both confirmed absent after checking standard paths and HTML; (7) ROPEA member data — site 403s from datacenter IPs (known logo-grid page; needs browser routing or Invest Europe cross-reference); (8) portal.just.ro, bpi.ro, arb.ro, mfinante apps, anevar.ro, unpir.ro, g4media/economedia feeds all actively refuse datacenter traffic — a recurring RO pattern meaning the ingestion plan should assume residential/browser-grade fetching for Romanian .ro government and association sites; (9) SeeNews and Emerging Europe regional coverage was not independently verified (403); (10) no Romanian sovereign wealth/privatization successor list beyond AMEPIP was located this session.

### RS — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Constraints first: the session's WebSearch budget was already exhausted (200/200) before this task started, so everything above was verified by direct fetch of known URLs; items I could not confirm by fetch are marked honestly. Absent or not-found: (1) National PE/VC association — no active Serbian Private Equity Association (SPEA-style) site could be confirmed; Serbia has no Invest Europe-affiliated national association; the gap is filled by SEC AIF/ManCo registers, Innovation Fund's Serbia Ventures program, DSI, and regional sources (Invest Europe CEE report, Vestbee). (2) EU ETS carbon registry national accounts — Serbia is not in the EU ETS; no national allowance registry exists (only future CBAM alignment); carbon category is genuinely absent. (3) MiCA licensee register — MiCA does not apply; the domestic Law on Digital Assets regime splits registers between NBS (virtual currencies) and SEC (digital tokens), both cataloged. (4) Standalone fund-administrator licensee list — none; depositary/custodian licensing sits inside the SEC registers. (5) UBO register — APR keeps Central Records of Beneficial Owners but public access is restricted (per project rule: never assume open UBO). (6) RICS Serbia — no national RICS directory; valuer roll is the MFIN licensed-appraiser register plus the national valuers association (NUPS), whose site I could not verify without search. (7) Leasing association (ALCS) — could not verify an active site; NBS lessor register covers the universe. (8) Unreachable during session despite likely existing: priv.rs (DNS dead — privatization moved under Ministry of Economy), boniteti.rs (connection refused), belex.rs / rgz.gov.rs / privreda.gov.rs / uos.rs / slglasnik.com (TLS certificate failures at our fetcher — several Serbian state sites use certificate chains our fetcher rejects; production crawler should retry with a proper trust store). (9) Beta news agency (beta.rs) — subscription wire, not verified, excluded rather than padded.

### SE — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched for but not found or not available: (1) a public bulk/API route for the FI company register — only the search UI plus free-reuse policy exists; the register must be crawled per category (MiCA, AIFM, etc.). (2) Exact Riksbank and FI RSS feed URLs — both document RSS subscription mechanisms but concrete feed URLs could not be extracted via fetch (bot/404 issues); needs one browser session to pin down. (3) A native di.se RSS feed that answers to non-browser clients — di.se blocks fetchers outright and third-party feed proxies have sprung up, suggesting the native feed is unreliable. (4) A public online Swedish court/insolvency case register — does not exist; bankruptcy events must come from PoIT (CAPTCHA-walled) and Bolagsverket status data. (5) Open bulk access to Euroclear Sweden share registers (ownership) — per-company paid extracts only. (6) A separate fund-administrator licensee list — fund administration is not separately licensed in Sweden; the FI register categories are the closest proxy. (7) Open bulk UBO data — Bolagsverket's beneficial-ownership register requires e-ID login, consistent with EU-wide access restrictions (per project law: never assume open UBO). (8) NGM/Spotlight bulk issuer downloads — HTML lists only. (9) An RSS or API for Nyhetsbyrån Direkt — the wire has no public site; its telegrams surface via Placera and brokers.

### SI — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

IMPORTANT CAVEAT: this session's WebSearch quota was exhausted (0 searches available), so discovery relied on the known Slovenian source landscape verified through ~30 direct URL fetches; a follow-up search pass could still surface additional niche portals (real-estate media, energy trade press, regional VC newsletters). Genuinely absent or blocked after checking: (1) No active national PE/VC association exists in Slovenia — no operating equivalent of Invest Europe at national level (the historical Slovenian venture-capital association is defunct); coverage is reconstructed from Business Angels of Slovenia, Startup Slovenia, Slovene Enterprise Fund and CEE-level directories. (2) No Slovenia-specific litigation-funding, royalties, ILS or tokenized-RWA registries or media were found — these niches have no dedicated SI sources. (3) No RICS Slovenia chapter directory — the statutory valuer roll lives at the Slovenian Institute of Auditors instead. (4) ATVP registers are machine-inaccessible (WAF 403 reconfirmed) — ESMA registers are the documented workaround. (5) Svet kapitala (Delo's capital-markets vertical) now redirects to Delo's error host — apparently discontinued as a standalone destination. (6) Delo, Dnevnik, Finance.si and Siol RSS endpoints could not be verified (blocked hosts, 404s, or error redirects) — rssUrl recorded only where the feed was actually confirmed (RTV, N1, Necenzurirano, SEOnet). (7) A 'Forbes Slovenia' edition could not be confirmed and was excluded rather than padded. (8) Note for gazette monitoring: Uradni list publication moved to PISRS on 1 Mar 2026 — pipelines must target PISRS going forward."

### SK — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Web search budget was exhausted for this session before the task began, so ALL verification was done by direct URL fetch (35+ fetches); nothing here was search-discovered beyond prior knowledge + hints. Confirmed absences/gaps: (1) No public RICS Slovakia member directory — valuer coverage routes through the Ministry of Justice znalec register instead. (2) No standalone Slovak factoring association found — factoring/ABL players sit under the leasing association (lizing.sk, currently serving a broken TLS cert) and NBS registers. (3) No separate national MiCA register — crypto licensees are a category inside NBS subjekty.nbs.sk (verified present). (4) No public Slovak national carbon-registry portal — SK EU ETS accounts are only browsable via the EU Union Registry public site. (5) ORSR itself exposes no API or bulk download — the machine route is the RPO API at api.statistics.sk. (6) RPVS (beneficial owners) has no documented public API. (7) energie-portal.sk, openiazoch.zoznam.sk and yimba.sk have no RSS feeds (checked pages/paths). (8) hnonline.sk, trend.sk, aktuality.sk, sita.sk, finreport.sk and the SME feed hub (rss.sme.sk, HTTP 402) block our fetcher — their RSS URLs are recorded where known but flagged unverified; retest with a browser UA before relying on them. (9) obcan.justice.sk registers (správcovia, znalci) render via JS — REPLIK is the parseable alternative for administrators. (10) AmCham member directory is login-gated. (11) investujeme.sk is dead as an independent portal (301 to Swiss Life Select) — excluded.

_XK competitor / intelligence-provider landscape (8 providers): see_ **docs/COMPETITOR-LANDSCAPE.md**.

### XK — searched but genuinely absent (honesty record)

Searched (web search budget was exhausted early in session, so verification was done by direct URL probing in Albanian and English) and NOT found, stated honestly: (1) Stock exchange — Kosovo has NO stock exchange, no listed-issuer regime and no CSD for equities; tier4 exchange coverage is structurally empty. (2) Securities/markets regulator — none separate; CBK is the unified regulator; there is no investment-fund (UCITS/AIF) register because no domestic fund industry exists beyond the two CBK-supervised pension vehicles. (3) PE/VC association — no national association exists (no Kosovo chapter of Invest Europe or a regional PEVCA); early-stage ecosystem mapping must go through ICK/STIKK instead. (4) Insurance association of Kosovo — referenced in sector literature but no working public website found (shs-ks.org, oshsk.org both unreachable); CBK's insurer register substitutes fully. (5) Crypto/VASP register — Kosovo's crypto-asset legal framework is recent and CBK does not yet publish a licensee register; nothing MiCA-grade exists (Kosovo is not an EU member). (6) Carbon registry — none; Kosovo is not in the EU ETS. (7) Valuer/appraiser directory — no RICS Kosovo chapter; property-appraiser licensing sits with the Ministry of Finance but no clean online roll was verified. (8) Dedicated insolvency register — none; insolvency runs through the Commercial Court, with decisions on the Cloudflare-protected judiciary portal (catalogued as js-blocked). (9) National open-data portal (opendata.rks-gov.net) — unreachable at check time (connection failure), so historical ARBK bulk datasets could not be confirmed. (10) KOSTT (transmission operator) website — unreachable at check time. (11) Litigation-funding, royalty, ILS or tokenization entities/registries — none found; Niche & Emerging Alts is an honest zero for Kosovo. (12) Nacionale.com RSS — feed 404; outlet excluded rather than padded. Total genuine catalogued sources: 48 (32 entities-side, 16 news-side), below the 50 target because the market is small — every entry above was probed live this session.
